Normalize to S:
```
def normalize(a,S):
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-12: return full uniform
   return a*S/s
```

Projection simplex implementation optimized:
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
   n=len(v)
   v=np.asarray(v)
   # use sort descending
   u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
   css=np.cumsum(u)
   rho=np.nonzero(u - (css-S)/np.arange(1,n+1) > 0)[0]
   if len(rho)==0: theta= (v.sum()-S)/n?; else rho=rho[-1]+1; theta=(u[:rho].sum()-S)/rho
   return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
Need handle S. Could use `valid = u >= (css - S)/idx`, but floating. Use parent implementation.

Pseudo:
```
a=normalize(a,S); y=a.copy(); t=1.0; step_scale=1.0
best=max(conv(a)); besta=a.copy(); it=0; last_improve=0
while time < deadline:
   it+=1
   beta = min(beta_max, beta_init * math.exp(beta_rate*it))
   q=conv(y); cur_max=q.max()
   if cur_max<best: update
   # weights full LSE
   w,lse_y = lse_weights(q,beta)
   grad = gradient_from_weights(y,w,fsize2)
   gn=norm(grad)
   if gn<1e-12: break
   step = step_scale / (2.0*beta + 2.0)
   # backtracking on LSE or max
   for back in range(10):
       x=project_simplex(y - step*grad,S)
       qx=conv(x); mx=qx.max()
       # compute lse_x quickly maybe from qx
       if mx < cur_max or lse_x < lse_y*0.999? accept
       step*=0.5
   if accepted:
      if momentum: tnew=...; y=x+...
      else y=x
      a=x
   else:
      # small random perturbation
   # periodically decay step_scale? increase if descent
```

```
def conv_self(a, fsize):
    A = rfft(a, fsize)
    c = irfft(A*A, fsize)
    return c[:2*len(a)-1]
```
For real, `A*A` not conjugate because linear convolution of a with a. Correct? `rfft(a)*rfft(a)` corresponds circular convolution of a with a (not a with conjugate) because FFT of a times FFT of a = FFT of circular convolution a*a. For real, yes. If we used `A*np.conj(A)` would give autocorrelation (power). We need convolution, so no conj.

For gradient cross-correlation with weights `w` length L=2n-1:
```
def weighted_conv_gradient(a, weights, fsize2):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    br = weights[::-1]
    A = rfft(a, fsize2); B = rfft(br, fsize2)
    y = irfft(A*B, fsize2)
    return 2.0 * y[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
Let's verify with n=2 example if `irfft` circular: fsize2>=3. br length padded. Good. The slice `y[n-1:2*n-1]` length n; reverse. For n=2 indices 1:3? 2*n-1=3, Python stop 3 gives [1,2], reverse -> [2,1]? Wait earlier m=2,1. For n=2 n-1=1, stop=2*n-1=3 includes y[1],y[2]. Reverse -> [y[2], y[1]], correct. For n=3 indices m=2,1,0? n-1=2, stop=5? length 3: m=2,3,4 reverse -> [4,3,2]. Formula m=2n-2-j: for j=0 m=4, j=1 m=3, j=2 m=2, correct. slice should be [n-1:2*n-1] length n (stops at 2n-1 exclusive). Good.

But linear convolution length n+L-1=3n-2, last index 3n-3. For n=3, 2n-1=5 exclusive gives 2,3,4 <=3n-3=5? 4 okay. Good.

Hinge weights: For target M, if no violation (all q<=M), we can't gradient. We lower M. Implementation:
```
def run_hinge(a0,S,goal,deadline,rng,n=...,fsize=...,fsize2=...):
    a=normalize(a0,S); y=a.copy(); best=max; t=0; M=min(best-1e-4, goal+0.002)
    if M<goal: M=goal
    step=0.05; step_scale=1.0; stagn=0
    while time:
       t+=1
       if t % 50 ==0: adapt M: if best < M: M = max(goal, 0.5*(best+M))? Actually if best much below M, lower M = max(goal, best - 0.0002).
       qy=maxy; if maxy<best update
       v=np.maximum(qy-M,0); sv=v.sum()
       if sv < 1e-12:
          # lower M
          M = max(goal, M - max(0.0002, 0.05*(M-goal)))
          continue
       weights=v/sv
       grad=weighted_conv_gradient(y,weights,fsize2)
       # step maybe scale with sv? Use step=step_scale/(1.0+10*(maxy-goal)?)
       step=step_scale*0.05
       accepted=False
       H2=0.5*np.dot(v,v)
       for bt in range(8):
          x=project_simplex(y-step*grad,S)
          qx=maxx; if maxx<best update
          vx=np.maximum(qx-M,0); H2x=0.5*dot(vx,vx)
          if maxx < maxy - 1e-10 or (H2x < H2 -1e-8 and maxx < maxy + 1e-8): accepted True
          step*=0.5
       if accepted:
          # plain or momentum? Use momentum small t? Maybe y = x - 0.9*(x-a)? To be safe, no momentum in hinge? Use y=x.copy(); a=x.
          a=x; y=x.copy(); step_scale=min(1.0, step_scale*1.02)
       else:
          # perturb or skip
       if best <= goal and time remaining: M=goal (or below)
       periodic noise if stagnation: y=project(y+noise)
```
V violation in q units; q max around1.5, sv maybe 0.01. H2 small. Step 0.05 maybe too large; backtrack.

We need update M based on current best to avoid M too low. If best - goal gap, set M = max(goal, best - 0.0005) initially. As best decreases, lower M. This chase.

Potential issue: If M too low, all q violate, weights normalized over many, gradient may push but cannot reach; then LSE needed. We can set M = min(best - 1e-4, goal + 0.001). If best is 1.506, M=1.504? Goal=1.50265, best-0.0005=1.5063? min with 1.50365 =>1.50365. So initial M near goal+0.001. This requires reducing max by 0.00315 in one M, maybe hard. Use gradual: M0 = best - 0.5*(best-goal)/10? Start M=best - 0.0005, then lower. If local can reduce 0.0005, continue. This incremental better. Set M = min(best - 0.0005, goal+0.0005) and lower by 10% of gap.

Hinge may struggle if a is already KKT for current max; lowering M creates violations at active lags, gradient tries reduce them but may increase others; H2 descent can find.

Maybe use `M = max(goal, 0.5*best + 0.5*goal + 0.0002)`? For initial 1.506, target 1.5027 => M=1.5046. Good.

Let's design `run_lse_fista` more concretely with time and objectives.

Detailed LSE FISTA:
```
def run_lse_fista(a0,S,deadline,rng,beta_init=20,beta_max=20000,beta_rate=0.01,step_factor=2.0,momentum=True,restart_every=2000):
    n=len(a0); fsize=next_pow2(2*n-1); fsize2=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    a=normalize(a0,S); y=a.copy(); t=1.0; step_scale=1.0
    q,maxobj=conv_and_max(a,fsize); best=maxobj; besta=a.copy(); it=0; last=0; no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       it+=1
       beta=min(beta_max,beta_init*(1.0+beta_rate*it)) maybe linear: beta = min(beta_max, beta_init + beta_rate*it)
       qy=conv_self(y,fsize); maxy=float(np.max(qy,0))
       if maxy<best: best=maxy; besta=y.copy(); last=it; no_improve=0; step_scale=min(2.0,step_scale*1.05)
       else: no_improve+=1
       w,lsey = lse_weights(qy,beta)
       grad=weighted_conv_gradient(y,w,fsize2)
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
       if gnorm<1e-12: break
       step=step_scale*step_factor/(2.0*beta+1.0) # maybe
       # scale gradient? If beta high, grad scale unaffected? grad=2corr(weights,a) O0.03. Step 2/beta small.
       accepted=False
       # Maybe use nonmonotone max and lse
       for _ in range(8):
          x=project_simplex(y - step*grad,S)
          qx=maxx=conv_and_max(x,fsize)
          if maxx < best: update
          if maxx < maxy - 1e-12:
             accepted=True; break
          # compute lse_x; if lse lower and maxx not too high
          # To avoid overflow, lse_value(qx,beta)
          lsex,_=lse_value_only(qx,beta)
          if lsex < lsey - 1e-10 and maxx <= maxy + 1e-8:
             accepted=True; break
          step *= 0.5
       if accepted:
          if momentum:
             tnew=0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4*t*t))
             ynew=x + ((t-1)/tnew)*(x-a)
             # project? ynew sum S if x,a sum S; but due numeric project
             y=project_simplex(ynew,S)  # projecting y may hurt acceleration but safe
             a=x; t=tnew
          else:
             a=x; y=x.copy(); t=1
       else:
          # perturb y to escape
          if no_improve > restart_every or rng.random()<0.01:
             noise=...; y=project_simplex(y+noise,S); a=y; t=1; no_improve=0
          else:
             # tiny step in negative grad without projection? skip
             pass
       if maxy <= goal? maybe can lower beta? 
    final conv a; update
    return besta,best
```
Projecting y to simplex each iter may reduce FISTA benefits but okay. In standard FISTA, y can be outside constraint after extrapolation but projected iterates inside; gradient evaluated at y outside may be okay? For constrained FISTA, y is extrapolated within? Usually y = x + β(x - previous) and can be outside constraint; one evaluates gradient at y (unconstrained) and then projects? For projection methods, yes y can be outside. But if y outside simplex, q may be larger; still can work. To avoid issues, project y to simplex? That makes y same as projection of extrapolate, reducing momentum. Previous code did `y_new = np.maximum(y_new,0)` but not project to sum? They didn't project y? Let's see parent: `if use_momentum: y_new = x_new + ...; y_new = np.maximum(y_new, 0.0); a=x_new; y=y_new; t_new`. They didn't enforce sum for y, only nonneg. Sum y = S if x_new,a sum S and coeff beta_y with β_y? Since y_new = (1+c)x_new - c a, c=(t-1)/t. Sum = S + c(S-S)=S if sums exact. Nonneg okay. So y remains simplex (if no negative). We can do same without projection, but clip negative to 0 and renormalize? Clipping changes sum. Better: if y has negatives, project to simplex. Sum should be S within error. We'll project y to simplex for safety.

Momentum coefficient c can be close to 1; y may have negative due cancellation; if clip changes sum, project.

Step factor: For β high, step small; if step too small, no progress. We can adapt based on best improvement: if max improved, step_scale *=1.05, if accepted but no improvement? Maybe.

Backup if no accepted: Try a coordinatewise shift? Could use `x=project_simplex(y - step*grad, S)` with tiny `step=1e-4/(beta+1)`. If still no accept, perturb.

LSE weights function efficient:
```
def lse_weights(q,beta):
   cmax=np.max(q)
   x=beta*(q-cmax)
   # x max=0, min maybe -large; exp stable
   ex=np.exp(x)  # underflow ok
   tot=ex.sum()
   w=ex/tot
   lse=cmax + np.log(tot)/beta
   return w,lse
```
If beta=0? no. For beta large and q-cmax differences small, ex ~1 for many if differences*beta small; okay.

Squared hinge weights:
```
def hinge_weights(q,M):
   v=np.maximum(q-M,0.0)
   s=v.sum()
   if s<=0: return None,0.0
   return v/s, 0.5*np.dot(v,v)
```

def spectral_perturb(a, amount, rng, fsize):
   A=rfft(a,fsize); phase=rng.normal(0,amount,fsize//2+1); phase[0]=0
   A_new=A*np.exp(1j*phase); new=irfft(A_new,fsize)[:n]; clip; normalize to S
```

Let's consider numerical `project_simplex` with S large 128. Input `v` may have huge negatives from step; sorting and theta. Implementation:
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    n = len(v)
    v = np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1,n+1)
    candidates = (css - S) / idx
    valid = candidates <= u
    if np.any(valid):
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) + 1)
    else: rho = n
    theta = (css[rho-1] - S) / rho
    out = v - theta
    out[out<0]=0
    # due numeric adjust sum?
    s=out.sum()
    if s>0:
       # maybe rescale if relative error
       if abs(s-S)>1e-9*max(1,S): out*=S/s
    return out
```
Parent used `valid = tc <= w`, note with descending. If none, rho=n, theta=(sum v -S)/n. If all v low. Good.

Scale after clipping could reintroduce negatives? Ifout min negative due multiply? out nonnegative before, scaling positive. Good.

def conv_and_max(a,fsize):
   c=conv_self(a,fsize)
   c_max = c.max()
   if c_max <0: c_max=0
   return c, float(c_max)
```

def objective_value(a):
    n=len(a); s=float(np.sum(a)); if s<0.01: return inf
    f=next_pow2(2*n-1); q=conv_self(a,f); mx=q.max(); return float(2*n*mx/(s*s))
```

Cache:
```
_FSIZE_CACHE={}
def get_fsize(n):
   size=2*n-1
   key=size
   if key not in _FSIZE_CACHE:
      _FSIZE_CACHE[key]=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
   return _FSIZE_CACHE[key]
def get_fsize2(n):
   size=3*n-2
   return 1 << (size-1).bit_length()
```
No issue.

Spectral perturb implementation with rfft:
```
def spectral_perturb(a, amount, rng, fsize):
    n=len(a); A=rfft(a,fsize); phase=rng.normal(0,amount,fsize//2+1); phase[0]=0
   B=A*np.exp(1j*phase)
   out=irfft(B,fsize)[:n]
   out=np.maximum(out,0)
   return normalize(out,S) # need S param
```
Need S param.

Random coordinate perturb:
```
def coordinate_perturb(a,S,rng,frac):
    scale=frac * (S / len(a))
    out=a + rng.normal(0,scale,len(a))
    return project_simplex(out,S)
```

Let's craft `run_lse_fista` in code carefully.

Pseudo code for run_lse_fista:
```
def run_lse_fista(a0,S,deadline,rng,beta_init=20.0,beta_max=20000.0,beta_rate=0.05,step_factor=5.0,momentum=True):
    n=len(a0); fsize=get_fsize(n); fsize2=get_fsize2(n)
    a=normalize(a0,S); y=a.copy(); t=1.0
    q,maxcur=conv_and_max(a,fsize); best=maxcur; besta=a.copy(); it=0; since=0
    step_scale=1.0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       now=time.monotonic()
       beta = min(beta_max, beta_init + beta_rate*it)  # linear
       qy=conv_self(y,fsize); maxy=float(qy.max())
       if maxy < best: ...
       w,_ = lse_weights(qy,beta)
       grad=weighted_gradient(y,w,fsize2)
       gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
       if gnorm <1e-12: break
       base_step = min(0.25, step_factor / max(1.0,beta))
       desired = 1e-4 / max(gnorm,1e-12)
       step = min(base_step, desired) * step_scale
       step=max(step,1e-6)
       accepted=False; accepted_x=None; accepted_max=None
       for _ in range(8):
           x=project_simplex(y - step*grad,S)
           qx=conv_self(x,fsize); maxx=float(qx.max())
           if maxx < best: best=maxx; besta=x.copy(); since=0
           if maxx < maxy - 1e-10:
               accepted=True; accepted_x=x; break
           # optional lse
           lsey = lse_value(qy,beta) # computed once outside
           lsex = lse_value(qx,beta)
           if lsex < lsey - 1e-10 and maxx <= maxy + 1e-8:
               accepted=True; accepted_x=x; break
           step *= 0.5
       if accepted:
           if momentum:
              tnew=0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4*t*t))
              ynew=x + ((t-1)/tnew)*(x-a)
              y=project_simplex(np.maximum(ynew,0),S)
              a=x; t=tnew
           else:
              a=x; y=x.copy(); t=1
           step_scale=min(2.0, step_scale*1.03)
       else:
           # tiny accepted? if maxy improved maybe accept x anyway? If no accepted, perturb
           if since > 500 or rng.random()<0.02:
               a=coordinate_perturb(a,S,rng,0.1)
               y=a.copy(); t=1.0; step_scale=1.0; since=0
           else:
               # do nothing, maybe a unchanged
               pass
    qf,maxf=conv_and_max(a,fsize); if maxf<best: ...
    return besta,best
```
But `lse_value(qy,beta)` should be computed once per iter before backtracks. For beta high, lsex compute exp; okay. Could skip lsex if maxy not decreased and beta>5000; but compute anyway for line search.

Important: `y` projected to simplex; gradient at y. If y == a due y projected, momentum lost. In standard, x and a in simplex; ynew in simplex if no negatives. Projecting to simplex only changes if negative/sum error. Sum ynew: x sum S, a sum S, coefficient c; sum S. Nonnegative? if some negative, projection changes. Usually fine.

`since` variable: update when best improved. In code, if maxy < best, since=0 else since +=1.

Beta schedule: `beta = min(beta_max, beta_init * math.exp(0.001*it))`? Linear may reach max quickly. We want start low, grow. Use exponential capped: `beta = min(beta_max, beta_init * (1.0 + 0.01*it))`? For it=1000 beta=20+? If beta_init=20, beta_max=20000; linear with rate 5: at 4000 reaches 20000. Use `beta = min(beta_max, beta_init + 2.0*it)`. For 10000 iterations beta~20020. Good. If time 60s ~ maybe 5000 iter; beta~10000. Good.

Can parameterize beta_init and max in calls. For final high polish, call with beta_init=500, beta_max=100000, momentum False, step_factor=2? Maybe.

Hinge function similar:
```
def run_hinge(a0,S,goal,deadline,rng, M_start=None, step0=0.05, perturb_frac=0.05):
   n...; a=normalize; y=a; t=1; best; M=...
   step_scale=1.0; since=0
   while time<deadline:
      it+=1
      qy=maxy; update best; if maxy<best M=max(goal-1e-4, min(M, maxy-0.0002))? keep
      if maxy <= M + 1e-6:
          # lower M
          newM = max(goal - 1e-4, min(M - 0.00025, maxy - 0.00005))
          M = newM
          t=1.0; y=a.copy(); step_scale=1.0 # reset momentum when M changes
          continue? Need compute gradient with new M; maybe not continue to save time? Continue.
      v=np.maximum(qy-M,0); sv=v.sum()
      if sv < 1e-12:
          M = max(goal - 1e-4, min(M - 0.00025, maxy - 0.00005)); continue
      w=v/sv
      grad=weighted_gradient(y,w,fsize2)
      gnorm; step=...
      H2y=0.5*dot(v,v)
      for bt:
         x=project(y-step*grad,S)
         qx,maxx=...
         vx=... H2x
         if maxx < maxy - 1e-10 or (H2x < H2y - 1e-10 and maxx < maxy + 1e-8): accept
         step*=0.5

Pseudo actual run_lse_fista:
```
def run_lse_fista(a0,S,deadline,rng,beta_init=20.0,beta_max=20000.0,beta_slope=5.0,step_factor=5.0,momentum=True):
    now=time.monotonic()
    if now >= deadline:
       a=normalize(a0,S); q,mx=conv_max(a,get_fsize(len(a))); return a,mx
    n=len(a0); fsize=get_fsize(n); fsize2=get_fsize2(n)
    a=normalize(a0,S); y=a.copy(); t=1.0
    q,best=conv_max(a,fsize); besta=a.copy(); it=0; since=0; step_scale=1.0
    move=0.01*S/n
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       beta = min(beta_max, beta_init + beta_slope*float(it))
       qy=conv_self(y,fsize); maxy=float(qy.max())
       if maxy < best - 1e-12:
          best=maxy; besta=y.copy(); since=0; step_scale=min(2.0, step_scale*1.03)
       else:
          since +=1
       w,lsey=lse_weights(qy,beta)
       grad=weighted_gradient(y,w,fsize2)
       gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
       if gnorm < 1e-12:
          break
       base_step = min(0.35, step_factor / max(1.0,beta))
       desired = move / max(gnorm,1e-12)
       step = min(base_step, desired) * step_scale
       if step < 1e-8: step=1e-8
       accepted_x=None
       for _ in range(10):
          x=project_simplex(y - step*grad,S)
          qx=conv_self(x,fsize); maxx=float(qx.max())
          if maxx < best -1e-12:
             best=maxx; besta=x.copy(); since=0
          if maxx < maxy - 1e-9:
             accepted_x=x; break
          lsex=lse_value(qx,beta)
          if lsex < lsey - 1e-9 and maxx <= maxy + 1e-7:
             accepted_x=x; break
          step *= 0.5
       if accepted_x is not None:
          x=accepted_x
          if momentum:
             tnew=0.5*(1.0+math.sqrt(1.0+4.0*t*t))
             ynew=x + ((t-1.0)/tnew)*(x-a)
             y=project_simplex(np.maximum(ynew,0.0),S)
             a=x
             t=tnew
          else:
             a=x; y=x.copy(); t=1.0
          step_scale=min(2.0, step_scale*1.02)
       else:
          if since > 300 or rng.random() < 0.02:
             frac = min(0.5, 0.02 + 0.01*min(since//100,10))
             a=coordinate_perturb(a,S,rng,frac)
             y=a.copy(); t=1.0; step_scale=1.0; since=0
          else:
             # do a very small step in negative gradient without line search? It may increase but maybe helps
             tiny = max(1e-6, step*0.01)
             a=project_simplex(y - tiny*grad,S)
             y=a.copy(); t=1.0
       # optional if best near target, stop? If best <= goal and beta high? We don't know goal here.
    qf,mf=conv_max(a,fsize)
    if mf < best: best=mf; besta=a.copy()
    return besta,best
```
Potential issue: If x improves best but accepted_x None? We update best to x but don't keep a; maybe not. If x improves best, likely accepted by max<maxy? Not necessarily if maxy is y's max, x max could be best but still >maxy? impossible if best was global lower. If x improves global but maxy not? maxy may be >best? Actually if best from previous lower than maxy, x < best and max < maxy, accepted likely. Fine.

`lse_value` used in backtrack; but `lse_value` computes exp each. If beta large and q-qmax negative large, exp underflow; okay. If `tot` maybe 0? exp(0) at max at least one, so >0.

In hinge similar but weights normalized violations. H2 compute: `H2y = 0.5 * float(np.dot(v,v))`. For line search, if v changes due x. Use `maxx <= maxy + 1e-7`.

Hinge function code:
```
def run_hinge(a0,S,goal,deadline,rng,M_start=None,step_factor=5.0,perturb_start=0.02):
   init now...
   a=normalize; y=a; t=1
   q,best=conv_max
   if M_start is None:
       if best <= goal: M=goal - 0.0002
       else: M=min(best - 0.0005, goal+0.001); M=max(goal-0.0002,M)
   if M < goal - 0.001: M=goal-0.0002
   move=0.01*S/n
   step_scale=1.0; since=0; it=0
   while time<deadline:
      it+=1
      qy,maxy=conv_self(y)
      update best
      if maxy <= M + 5e-4:
         newM = max(goal - 0.0003, min(M - 0.00025, maxy - 0.00002))
         if newM < M:
             M = newM; t=1.0; y=a.copy(); step_scale=1.0; since=0
             # maybe if M <= goal-... and best <= goal, continue to lower? 
             continue? We should not continue to avoid infinite if M lower but still maxy? It will compute next iter. Continue uses no gradient, but can loop rapidly if maxy below newM? If maxy is much below M, newM might still >maxy, next loop lower again. Could be many loops without optimization, but cheap? It recomputes q each loop, could be 10000 lowering. Limit lower step. Better not continue; set M and compute gradient with new M, v may be zero if maxy<M; then lower again next if sv zero. This can rapidly lower M with just conv per iter. That's okay if we only lower when all q below M; if M is far above max, lowering by 0.00025 per iter, gap 0.01 requires 40 convs, trivial. Continue to next iteration; no need immediate. We can not continue, just update M and then compute v; if sv zero, lower again next iteration.
      v=np.maximum(qy-M,0); sv=...
      if sv < 1e-12:
          # lower M if possible
          if M > goal - 0.0003:
              M = max(goal - 0.0003, M - 0.00025)
              t=1; y=a.copy(); step_scale=1; since=0
          else:
              # at minimum, maybe no violations; if best <= goal, can stop? Let polarizing LSE? Return if max <= goal.
              if maxy <= goal + 1e-9:
                  break
          continue
      w=v/sv; grad=...
      step=...
      H2y=0.5*dot(v,v)
      accepted none
      for bt:
         x=project(y-step*grad); qx,maxx; vx; H2x
         update best
         if maxx < maxy -1e-9 or (H2x < H2y -1e-10 and maxx <= maxy+1e-7): accept
         step*=0.5
      if accept:
         momentum...
         step_scale small up
         since=0
      else:
         perturb if since>300...
   final
```
If M reaches goal-0.0003 and still violations (max>goal), weights gradient tries reduce to below goal. If cannot, will perturb repeatedly. Good.

For final polishing, set M_start maybe `min(global_c - 0.0002, GOAL - 0.0002)` if global_c>GOAL? We want push below. If global_c=1.505, M_start=1.50245? That's below goal by 0.0002? Actually GOAL=1.50265, GOAL-0.0002=1.50245; if global_c-0.0002=1.5048, min=1.50245. Should we target that low? Maybe too aggressive. Use M_start = max(GOAL - 0.0001, min(global_c-0.0005, GOAL+0.0005)). For final, GOAL-0.0001=1.50255; but eval target 1.5027, margin 0.00015. Good. If internal reaches GOAL-0.0001, fine. Use `goal = 1.5026`.

Set `GOAL = 1.5026` and `M_MIN = GOAL - 0.0002 = 1.5024`. Might be below target enough but not too impossible. If cannot, still if best <1.5027 returned. If hinge chases 1.5024 and fails, it may perturb and not improve; but phase time limited. We can in final call hinge with `M_start=GOAL+0.0001` and `goal=GOAL`; if success already. Need not chase below.

Maybe define `TARGET = 1.5027`, `POLISH_GOAL = 1.5026`. In run_hinge, goal=POLISH_GOAL. If best <= POLISH_GOAL, stop or chase lower? We can stop to save time; final verify. But if internal error, maybe need slightly lower. Use `M_MIN = goal - 0.0001`. Fine.

Now, run_adam_lse implementation details:
```
def run_adam_lse(a0,S,deadline,rng,beta_init=50,beta_max=10000,beta_slope=2.0,lr_init=0.002):
   ... m,v zero, t=1
   while time:
      it+=1; beta=min(...)
      q,maxy; update best
      w=...; grad=...
      m=0.9*m+0.1*grad; v=0.999*v+0.001*grad*grad
      mhat=m/(1-0.9**it); vhat=...
      progress=(time-start)/max_total maybe; lr=lr_init*(1-0.8*progress) + 1e-5
      # limit update norm
      upd = lr*mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
      max_upd = 0.005 * (S/n)  # 0.1% coord
      if norm(upd) > max_upd: upd *= max_upd/norm
      a=project_simplex(a-upd,S)
      if every 2000 and rng.random()<0.05: perturb and reset m,v
   final
```
This is stable. Use `norm(upd)` per iter; perhaps heavy but okay.

Maybe skip Adam if not needed. But cycles can use Adam mode occasionally. Good.

def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400.0):
    start=time.monotonic(); budget=max(0.0,float(budget_s)); deadline=start+budget-20

   time_left=...

   # choose n

       if n2 <= 32768: n=n2

   init = resample_sequence(global_a,n) if len(global_a)!=n else global_a.copy()
   # perturb
   iteration+=1
   strength = min(0.6, 0.02 + 0.03*fail + 0.1*((iteration%5)/5.0))
   mode = rng.random()
   if mode <0.4:
       init=coordinate_perturb(init,S,rng,strength)
   elif mode<0.65:
       fsize=get_fsize(n)
       init=spectral_perturb(init,S,rng,amount=0.05+0.05*strength,fsize=fsize)
   elif mode<0.8:
       shift=int(rng.integers(1,n))
       init=np.roll(init,shift)
       init=normalize(init,S)
   else:
       shift=int(rng.integers(1,n))
       init=0.5*init +0.5*np.roll(init,shift)
       init=normalize(init,S)
   # phase budget
   phase_left = min(120.0, time_left - 300.0)
   # maybe if time_left > 600, phase 150

       # LSE

              update
    fail if no improve
    # if global_c <= GOAL and fail high, break? Continue maybe lower.
```

_FSIZE_CACHE = {}
def get_fsize(n):
   size = 2*n - 1
   if size <=0: return 1
   p = 1 << (size-1).bit_length()
   _FSIZE_CACHE[size]=p
   return p
```

def run_phase(a0,S,phase_deadline,rng,goal,lse_frac=0.45,hinge_frac=0.50,adam_frac=0.0,beta_init=30.0,beta_max=20000.0,beta_slope=4.0,momentum=True):

       a=normalize(a0,S); return a, objective_value(a)
    a=normalize(a0,S); best_c=objective_value(a); best_a=a.copy()
    total = phase_deadline - start
    if lse_frac > 0 and total > 10:
       end = min(phase_deadline, start + total*lse_frac)
       if end - time.monotonic() > 5:
          a,c=run_lse_fista(a,S,end,rng,beta_init=beta_init,beta_max=beta_max,beta_slope=beta_slope,momentum=momentum)
          if np.isfinite(c) and c < best_c: best_c=c; best_a=a.copy()
    if hinge_frac > 0:
       # use up to remaining*maybe 0.9 or total*hinge_frac

       if end - time.monotonic() > 5:
          a,c=run_hinge(a,S,goal,end,rng)
          update
    if adam_frac >0:
       rem=... t=min(rem*0.5, total*adam_frac) ...
    # ensure best_a corresponds best_c? If local a after LSE improved but global update then hinge didn't return that? We update best_a in each.
    return best_a,best_c

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
with internal normalization sum(a) = sqrt(2*n), so the score is
simply max(a*a).

The implementation combines:
  - full log-sum-exp smoothed max-coefficient gradient optimization (FISTA-like),
  - squared-hinge / target-bound polishing,
  - Adam exploration in a log-sum-exp surrogate,
  - random coordinate / spectral / cyclic restarts and resampling.

The helper functions are intentionally top-level; no closures or lambdas are used.
"""

import math
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft


_FSIZE_CACHE = {}


def get_fsize(n):
    size = 2 * int(n) - 1
    if size <= 0:
        return 1
    val = _FSIZE_CACHE.get(size)
    if val is None:
        val = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
        _FSIZE_CACHE[size] = val
    return int(val)


def get_fsize2(n):
    size = 3 * int(n) - 2
    if size <= 0:
        return 1
    val = _FSIZE_CACHE.get(size)
    if val is None:
        val = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
        _FSIZE_CACHE[size] = val
    return int(val)


def conv_self(a, fsize):
    n = len(a)
    A = rfft(a, fsize)
    c = irfft(A * A, fsize)
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def conv_max(a, fsize):
    c = conv_self(a, fsize)
    m = float(c.max())
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return c, m


def objective_value(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return float("inf")
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = int(a.size)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    fsize = get_fsize(n)
    c, mx = conv_max(a, fsize)
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def normalize(a, S):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0 or s < 1e-14:
        return np.full(n, S / max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a * (S / s)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s2 = float(a.sum())
    if s2 > 0.0 and abs(s2 - S) > 1e-10 * max(1.0, abs(S)):
        a = a * (S / s2)
    return a


def project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(v.size)
    if n == 0:
        return v.copy()
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cand = (css - S) / idx
    valid = cand <= u
    if np.any(valid):
        rho = int(np.max(np.nonzero(valid)[0]) + 1)
    else:
        rho = n
    theta = (css[rho - 1] - S) / rho
    out = v - theta
    out[out < 0.0] = 0.0
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        if abs(s - S) > 1e-10 * max(1.0, abs(S)):
            out = out * (S / s)
    else:
        out = np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return out


def resample_sequence(a, n_target):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n_target = int(n_target)
    if a.size == n_target:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size <= 1:
        return np.full(n_target, float(a[0]) if a.size else 0.0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n_target <= 1:
        return a[:1].copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_target)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    out[out < 0.0] = 0.0
    return out


def weighted_gradient(a, weights, fsize2):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k weights[k] * q_k where q = convolve(a,a),
    with weights as an array of length 2*n-1.
    """
    n = int(len(a))
    wrev = weights[::-1]
    A = rfft(a, fsize2)
    B = rfft(wrev, fsize2)
    y = irfft(A * B, fsize2)
    return 2.0 * y[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]


def lse_weights(q, beta):
    cmax = float(q.max())
    x = beta * (q - cmax)
    e = np.exp(x)
    tot = float(e.sum())
    if tot <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(q)
        w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
        return w, cmax
    w = e / tot
    lse = cmax + math.log(max(tot, 1e-300)) / beta
    return w, float(lse)


def lse_value(q, beta):
    cmax = float(q.max())
    x = beta * (q - cmax)
    e = np.exp(x)
    tot = float(e.sum())
    if tot <= 1e-300:
        return cmax
    return float(cmax + math.log(tot) / beta)


def coordinate_perturb(a, S, rng, frac):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(a.size)
    scale = float(frac) * (S / max(1, n))
    if scale <= 0.0:
        return a.copy()
    out = a + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n)
    return project_simplex(out, S)


def spectral_perturb(a, S, rng, amount, fsize):
    n = int(len(a))
    A = rfft(a, fsize)
    phase = rng.normal(0.0, float(amount), fsize // 2 + 1)
    phase[0] = 0.0
    if phase.size > 1:
        phase[-1] = 0.0
    B = A * np.exp(1j * phase)
    out = irfft(B, fsize)[:n]
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return normalize(out, S)


def run_lse_fista(a0, S, deadline, rng,
                  beta_init=20.0, beta_max=20000.0, beta_slope=4.0,
                  step_factor=5.0, momentum=True):
    now = time.monotonic()
    if now >= deadline:
        a = normalize(a0, S)
        _, m = conv_max(a, get_fsize(len(a)))
        return a, m

    n = int(len(a0))
    fsize = get_fsize(n)
    fsize2 = get_fsize2(n)
    a = normalize(a0, S)
    y = a.copy()
    t = 1.0
    _, best = conv_max(a, fsize)
    best_a = a.copy()
    it = 0
    since = 0
    step_scale = 1.0
    move = 0.01 * S / max(1, n)

    start = now
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        beta = min(float(beta_max), float(beta_init) + float(beta_slope) * float(it))
        qy = conv_self(y, fsize)
        maxy = float(qy.max())
        if maxy < best - 1e-12:
            best = maxy
            best_a = y.copy()
            since = 0
            step_scale = min(2.0, step_scale * 1.03)
        else:
            since += 1

        w, lsey = lse_weights(qy, beta)
        grad = weighted_gradient(y, w, fsize2)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm < 1e-12:
            break

        base_step = min(0.35, float(step_factor) / max(1.0, beta))
        desired = move / max(gnorm, 1e-12)
        step = min(base_step, desired) * step_scale
        if step < 1e-8:
            step = 1e-8

        accepted_x = None
        for _ in range(10):
            x = project_simplex(y - step * grad, S)
            qx = conv_self(x, fsize)
            maxx = float(qx.max())
            if maxx < best - 1e-12:
                best = maxx
                best_a = x.copy()
                since = 0
            if maxx < maxy - 1e-9:
                accepted_x = x
                break
            lsex = lse_value(qx, beta)
            if lsex < lsey - 1e-9 and maxx <= maxy + 1e-7:
                accepted_x = x
                break
            step *= 0.5

        if accepted_x is not None:
            x = accepted_x
            if momentum:
                tnew = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
                ynew = x + ((t - 1.0) / tnew) * (x - a)
                y = project_simplex(np.maximum(ynew, 0.0), S)
                a = x
                t = tnew
            else:
                a = x
                y = x.copy()
                t = 1.0
            step_scale = min(2.0, step_scale * 1.02)
        else:
            frac = min(0.5, 0.02 + 0.01 * min(since // 100, 10))
            if since > 300 or rng.random() < 0.02:
                a = coordinate_perturb(a, S, rng, frac)
                y = a.copy()
                t = 1.0
                step_scale = 1.0
                since = 0
            else:
                tiny = max(1e-6, step * 0.01)
                a = project_simplex(y - tiny * grad, S)
                y = a.copy()
                t = 1.0

    _, final_m = conv_max(a, fsize)
    if final_m < best:
        best = final_m
        best_a = a.copy()
    return best_a, float(best)


def run_hinge(a0, S, goal, deadline, rng, M_start=None, step_factor=5.0):
    now = time.monotonic()
    if now >= deadline:
        a = normalize(a0, S)
        _, m = conv_max(a, get_fsize(len(a)))
        return a, m

    n = int(len(a0))
    fsize = get_fsize(n)
    fsize2 = get_fsize2(n)
    a = normalize(a0, S)
    y = a.copy()
    t = 1.0
    _, best = conv_max(a, fsize)
    best_a = a.copy()

    goal = float(goal)
    m_min = goal - 0.0002
    if M_start is None:
        if best <= goal:
            M = max(m_min, best - 0.0001)
        else:
            M = min(best - 0.0005, goal + 0.0010)
            M = max(m_min, M)
    else:
        M = max(m_min, float(M_start))

    it = 0
    since = 0
    step_scale = 1.0
    move = 0.01 * S / max(1, n)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        qy = conv_self(y, fsize)
        maxy = float(qy.max())
        if maxy < best - 1e-12:
            best = maxy
            best_a = y.copy()
            since = 0

        if maxy <= M + 1e-6:
            newM = max(m_min, min(M - 0.00025, maxy - 0.00005))
            if newM < M:
                M = newM
                t = 1.0
                y = a.copy()
                step_scale = 1.0
                since = 0

        v = np.maximum(qy - M, 0.0)
        sv = float(v.sum())
        if sv < 1e-12:
            if M > m_min:
                M = max(m_min, M - 0.00025)
                t = 1.0
                y = a.copy()
                step_scale = 1.0
                since = 0
            else:
                if maxy <= goal + 1e-8:
                    break
                if since > 80 or rng.random() < 0.05:
                    a = coordinate_perturb(a, S, rng, min(0.25, 0.02 + 0.01 * min(since // 20, 10)))
                    y = a.copy()
                    t = 1.0
                    step_scale = 1.0
                    since = 0
            continue

        w = v / sv
        grad = weighted_gradient(y, w, fsize2)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm < 1e-12:
            continue

        base_step = min(0.35, float(step_factor) / max(1.0, 1.0))
        desired = move / max(gnorm, 1e-12)
        step = min(base_step, desired) * step_scale
        if step < 1e-8:
            step = 1e-8

        H2y = 0.5 * float(np.dot(v, v))
        accepted_x = None
        for _ in range(10):
            x = project_simplex(y - step * grad, S)
            qx = conv_self(x, fsize)
            maxx = float(qx.max())
            if maxx < best - 1e-12:
                best = maxx
                best_a = x.copy()
                since = 0
            vx = np.maximum(qx - M, 0.0)
            H2x = 0.5 * float(np.dot(vx, vx))
            if maxx < maxy - 1e-9:
                accepted_x = x
                break
            if H2x < H2y - 1e-10 and maxx <= maxy + 1e-7:
                accepted_x = x
                break
            step *= 0.5

        if accepted_x is not None:
            x = accepted_x
            tnew = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
            ynew = x + ((t - 1.0) / tnew) * (x - a)
            y = project_simplex(np.maximum(ynew, 0.0), S)
            a = x
            t = tnew
            step_scale = min(2.0, step_scale * 1.02)
        else:
            frac = min(0.4, 0.02 + 0.01 * min(since // 50, 10))
            if since > 200 or rng.random() < 0.03:
                a = coordinate_perturb(a, S, rng, frac)
                y = a.copy()
                t = 1.0
                step_scale = 1.0
                since = 0
            else:
                tiny = max(1e-6, step * 0.01)
                a = project_simplex(y - tiny * grad, S)
                y = a.copy()
                t = 1.0

    _, final_m = conv_max(a, fsize)
    if final_m < best:
        best = final_m
        best_a = a.copy()
    return best_a, float(best)


def run_adam_lse(a0, S, deadline, rng,
                 beta_init=50.0, beta_max=20000.0, beta_slope=2.0,
                 lr_init=0.01):
    now = time.monotonic()
    if now >= deadline:
        a = normalize(a0, S)
        _, m = conv_max(a, get_fsize(len(a)))
        return a, m

    n = int(len(a0))
    fsize = get_fsize(n)
    fsize2 = get_fsize2(n)
    a = normalize(a0, S)
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    vv = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    it = 0
    _, best = conv_max(a, fsize)
    best_a = a.copy()
    max_update = 0.02 * S / max(1, n)
    start = now

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        beta = min(float(beta_max), float(beta_init) + float(beta_slope) * float(it))
        q = conv_self(a, fsize)
        mx = float(q.max())
        if mx < best - 1e-12:
            best = mx
            best_a = a.copy()

        w, _ = lse_weights(q, beta)
        grad = weighted_gradient(a, w, fsize2)

        m = 0.9 * m + 0.1 * grad
        vv = 0.999 * vv + 0.001 * (grad * grad)
        bi1 = max(1e-12, 1.0 - math.pow(0.9, it))
        bi2 = max(1e-12, 1.0 - math.pow(0.999, it))
        mhat = m / bi1
        vhat = vv / bi2

        frac = max(0.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) / max(1e-9, deadline - start))
        lr = float(lr_init) * (0.2 + 0.8 * frac)
        upd = lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)
        un = float(np.linalg.norm(upd))
        if un > max_update and un > 0.0:
            upd *= max_update / un
        a = project_simplex(a - upd, S)

        if it % 2500 == 0 and rng.random() < 0.06:
            a = coordinate_perturb(a, S, rng, 0.05 + 0.02 * rng.random())
            m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            vv = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            it = max(1, it // 3)

    _, final_m = conv_max(a, fsize)
    if final_m < best:
        best = final_m
        best_a = a.copy()
    return best_a, float(best)


def run_phase(a0, S, phase_deadline, rng, goal,
              lse_frac=0.45, hinge_frac=0.50, adam_frac=0.0,
              beta_init=30.0, beta_max=20000.0, beta_slope=4.0,
              momentum=True):
    start = time.monotonic()
    if start >= phase_deadline:
        a = normalize(a0, S)
        return a, objective_value(a)

    a = normalize(a0, S)
    best_c = objective_value(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    total = max(0.0, phase_deadline - start)

    if lse_frac > 0 and total > 10:
        t = max(0.0, total * float(lse_frac))
        end = min(phase_deadline, time.monotonic() + t)
        if end - time.monotonic() > 5:
            a, c = run_lse_fista(a, S, end, rng,
                                 beta_init=beta_init,
                                 beta_max=beta_max,
                                 beta_slope=beta_slope,
                                 momentum=momentum)
            if np.isfinite(c) and c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_a = a.copy()

    if hinge_frac > 0:
        rem = max(0.0, phase_deadline - time.monotonic())
        t = min(rem, total * float(hinge_frac))
        end = min(phase_deadline, time.monotonic() + t)
        if end - time.monotonic() > 5:
            a, c = run_hinge(a, S, goal, end, rng)
            if np.isfinite(c) and c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_a = a.copy()

    if adam_frac > 0:
        rem = max(0.0, phase_deadline - time.monotonic())
        t = min(rem, total * float(adam_frac))
        end = min(phase_deadline, time.monotonic() + t)
        if end - time.monotonic() > 5:
            a, c = run_adam_lse(a, S, end, rng)
            if np.isfinite(c) and c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_a = a.copy()

    _, last_c = conv_max(best_a, get_fsize(len(best_a)))
    if last_c < best_c:
        best_c = last_c
    return best_a, float(best_c)


def make_fallback(n, rng):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 4:
        a += 0.25 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x / max(2.0, n / 4.0))
        a += 0.10 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x / max(2.0, n / 7.0))
        a += rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, n)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return a


def uniform_sequence(n):
    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    return np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)


def load_base_sequence():
    seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if seq is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        return None
    arr = arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
    if arr.size < 2:
        return None
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return arr


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not math.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0:
        budget = 2400.0
    deadline = start + max(0.0, budget - 25.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    EXTERNAL_TARGET = 1.5027
    GOAL = 1.5026

    base = load_base_sequence()
    if base is None:
        main_n = 8192
        base = make_fallback(main_n, rng)
    else:
        L = int(base.size)
        if 1024 <= L <= 16384:
            main_n = L
        else:
            main_n = 8192
        if main_n > 16384:
            main_n = 16384

    # Initial working point.
    global_a = resample_sequence(base, main_n) if len(base) != main_n else base.astype(np.float64).copy()
    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * main_n)
    global_a = normalize(global_a, S)
    global_c = objective_value(global_a)
    initial_c = global_c
    if not np.isfinite(global_c):
        global_a = uniform_sequence(main_n)
        S = math.sqrt(2.0 * main_n)
        global_invalid_c = objective_value(global_a)
        if not np.isfinite(global_c):
            return [float(x) for x in global_a]
    print("Initial length=%d objective=%.6f" % (main_n, global_c))

    # Quick symmetric/shifted/blended variants.
    tried_shifts = set()
    for frac in (0.25, 1.0 / 3.0, 0.5, 2.0 / 3.0, 0.1, 0.9):
        sh = int(round(frac * main_n)) % main_n
        if sh == 0 or sh in tried_shifts:
            continue
        tried_shifts.add(sh)

        a = np.roll(global_a, sh)
        a = normalize(a, S)
        c = objective_value(a)
        if np.isfinite(c) and c < global_c - 1e-12:
            global_c = c
            global_a = a.copy()
            print("Shift %d improved to %.6f" % (sh, global_c))

        a = 0.5 * global_a + 0.5 * np.roll(global_a, sh)
        a = normalize(a, S)
        c = objective_value(a)
        if np.isfinite(c) and c < global_c - 1e-12:
            global_c = c
            global_a = a.copy()
            print("Blend shift %d improved to %.6f" % (sh, global_c))

        a = 0.8 * global_a + 0.2 * np.roll(global_a, sh)
        a = normalize(a, S)
        c = objective_value(a)
        if np.isfinite(c) and c < global_c - 1e-12:
            global_c = c
            global_a = a.copy()
            print("80/20 blend shift %d improved to %.6f" % (sh, global_c))

    # Initial focused polish on the main length.
    time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if time_left > 260:
        dur = min(210.0, time_left - 330.0)
        if dur > 40:
            end = time.monotonic() + dur
            a, c = run_phase(
                global_a, S, end, rng, GOAL,
                lse_frac=0.40, hinge_frac=0.60, adam_frac=0.0,
                beta_init=20.0, beta_max=8000.0, beta_slope=4.0,
                momentum=True
            )
            if np.isfinite(c) and c < global_c - 1e-12:
                global_c = c
                global_a = a.copy()
                print("Initial phase improved to %.6f" % global_c)

    # Main search loop: repeated short phases with random restarts/resampling.
    fail_count = 0
    iteration = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 240:
        time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if time_left <= 180:
            break

        # Choose a working length.
        n_work = main_n
        r = rng.random()
        if time_left > 1200 and r < 0.16 and main_n * 2 <= 16384:
            n_work = main_n * 2
        elif time_left > 800 and r < 0.32 and max(2048, main_n // 2) <= 16384:
            n_work = max(2048, main_n // 2)

        S_work = math.sqrt(2.0 * n_work)
        init = resample_sequence(global_a, n_work) if len(global_a) != n_work else global_a.copy()
        S_work = math.sqrt(2.0 * len(init))

        iteration += 1
        strength = min(0.60, 0.02 + 0.035 * fail_count + 0.08 * ((iteration % 5) / 5.0))

        mode = rng.random()
        if mode < 0.40:
            init = coordinate_perturb(init, S_work, rng, strength)
        elif mode < 0.62:
            fsize = get_fsize(len(init))
            init = spectral_perturb(init, S_work, rng, amount=0.05 + 0.10 * strength, fsize=fsize)
        elif mode < 0.78:
            sh = int(rng.integers(1, len(init)))
            init = np.roll(init, sh)
            init = normalize(init, S_work)
        else:
            sh = int(rng.integers(1, len(init)))
            init = 0.5 * init + 0.5 * np.roll(init, sh)
            init = normalize(init, S_work)

        init_c = objective_value(init)
        if np.isfinite(init_c) and init_c < global_c - 1e-12:
            global_c = init_c
            global_a = init.copy()
            fail_count = 0
            print("Perturbed start improved to %.6f (n=%d)" % (global_c, len(init)))

        # Phase budget.
        time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if time_left <= 180:
            break
        phase_budget = min(140.0, time_left - 300.0)
        if init_c > global_c + 0.02:
            phase_budget = min(70.0, phase_budget)
        if global_c <= GOAL + 0.0002:
            phase_budget = min(80.0, phase_budget)
        if phase_budget <= 25:
            continue

        cycle_end = time.monotonic() + phase_budget
        if global_c <= GOAL + 0.0002:
            a, c = run_phase(
                init, S_work, cycle_end, rng, GOAL,
                lse_frac=0.18, hinge_frac=0.82, adam_frac=0.0,
                beta_init=100.0, beta_max=60000.0, beta_slope=10.0,
                momentum=False
            )
        else:
            a, c = run_phase(
                init, S_work, cycle_end, rng, GOAL,
                lse_frac=0.42, hinge_frac=0.50,
                adam_frac=(0.08 if iteration % 3 == 0 else 0.0),
                beta_init=30.0, beta_max=25000.0, beta_slope=5.0,
                momentum=True
            )

        if np.isfinite(c) and c < global_c - 1e-12:
            global_c = c
            global_a = a.copy()
            fail_count = 0
            print("Cycle %d (n=%d) improved to %.6f" % (iteration, len(global_a), global_c))
        else:
            fail_count += 1

    # Final intensive polish on the best sequence found.
    time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if len(global_a) > 0 and time_left > 40:
        S_best = math.sqrt(2.0 * len(global_a))
        if time_left > 70:
            end = min(deadline - 10, time.monotonic() + min(55.0, time_left - 30))
            a, c = run_lse_fista(
                global_a, S_best, end, rng,
                beta_init=300.0, beta_max=80000.0, beta_slope=20.0,
                step_factor=3.0, momentum=False
            )
            if np.isfinite(c) and c < global_c - 1e-12:
                global_c = c
                global_a = a.copy()
                print("Final LSE polished to %.6f" % global_c)

        time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if time_left > 50:
            m_start = None
            if global_c > GOAL:
                m_start = max(GOAL - 0.0002, min(global_c - 0.0005, GOAL + 0.0005))
            end = min(deadline - 5, time.monotonic() + min(140.0, time_left - 10))
            a, c = run_hinge(global_a, S_best, GOAL, end, rng, M_start=m_start)
            if np.isfinite(c) and c < global_c - 1e-12:
                global_c = c
                global_a = a.copy()
                print("Final hinge polished to %.6f" % global_c)

    # If the best is large, consider a safer smaller representation.
    try:
        if len(global_a) > 8192:
            small_n = 8192
            small_a = resample_sequence(global_a, small_n)
            small_S = math.sqrt(2.0 * small_n)
            small_a = normalize(small_a, small_S)
            small_c = objective_value(small_a)
            if np.isfinite(small_c) and small_c < EXTERNAL_TARGET - 1e-7 and small_c < global_c + 0.0012:
                global_a = small_a
                global_c = small_c
                print("Satified to length %d with objective %.6f" % (small_n, global_c))
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Final sanitization and verification.
    global_a = np.asarray(global_a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if global_a.size == 0:
        global_a = uniform_sequence(8192)

    global_a = np.clip(global_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S_out = math.sqrt(2.0 * len(global_a))
    global_a = normalize(global_a, S_out)
    if np.sum(global_a) < 0.01:
        global_a = uniform_sequence(8192)

    final_list = [float(x) for x in global_a]
    try:
        ver = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        if np.isfinite(ver):
            global_c = float(ver)
    except Exception:
        pass

    print("Best length=%d, internal=%s, evaluator=%s" %
          (len(final_list),
           np.format_float_positional(global_c, trim="-") if np.isfinite(global_c) else "inf",
           np.format_float_positional(global_c, trim="-") if np.isfinite(global_c) else "inf"))

    return final_list
```
